It just pulls me off course.\n\nDoctor: When you get distracted and switch tasks like that, does it feel like your attention is pulled away automatically, almost like a reflex, or do you feel like you're choosing to shift focus because you think something else is more important or interesting?\n\nPatient: I don't know... it's just... I just switch. I don't really think about if it's a reflex or a choice. I just... start doing the other thing.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel restless, as if you need to be doing something all the time, or find yourself having trouble staying still?\n\nPatient: I guess so. Um... lately, there's just been this... humming current in me, I suppose. From the moment I wake up. I just keep moving. Rearranging the studio, drafting proposals, emailing people... fussing with my supplies. Sitting quietly just... it felt almost impossible. So I just kept doing things.\n\nDoctor: Can you think of a time when this restlessness or need to keep doing things caused you to act quickly without thinking, like making impulsive decisions or interrupting others?\n\nPatient: I don't really know. I don't think I interrupt people.\n\nDoctor: Have you had trouble staying focused or organizing tasks in parts of your life outside of work, like at home or in social situations, and have these kinds of problems been happening for a long time, maybe even since you were a child?\n\nPatient: I don't think so.
